## 32. Registered Drafts at Outer Settings Dismissal — Partial Protection

This increment starts from `bfe5184c2c61a6bc57a5956bbe733fbb8931053d`. Its scope is **outer Settings Close/Escape for existing built-in credential inputs, Access and scoped Defaults**. Category navigation still preserves mounted forms without configuration writes. Other provider/model/plugin/MCP/profile/rename/OAuth drafts, internal scope/shared-editor replacement, browser history/unload and global session/palette navigation are **not yet guarded**. This is not complete dirty-edit protection or a new Save transaction.

`settings-drafts.tsx` keeps only ephemeral dirty flags in a Settings-local `Set<symbol>`; values and baselines remain in their owning forms. Credential baselines start as the existing empty/mask values and update only after successful explicit saves. Access's baseline updates only on explicit Apply, retaining its original canonical per-tab authentication semantics. Cleared/incomplete values remain drafts even when the original Save handler cannot submit them. Defaults reuses its existing dirty fields. No API, save validation, payload, credential persistence, permission, consent or budget changes are introduced.

Outer dismissal now opens a nonblocking nested `alertdialog` with explicit Keep editing/Discard edits, inherited focus/Escape leases, 44px controls and inset semantic focus. Only header/business content becomes inert; the confirmation remains inside its Settings modal's DOM ownership. Cancelling preserves all drafts; saving one owner cannot clear another's flag. Explicit Discard neither saves edits nor cancels an already-started save, and the warning says such saves may still finish. Workspace/cwd/trust replacement clears confirmation and immediately replaces the existing business body/flags. App's internal `onEscape(close)` callback preserves pending-palette cancellation without requesting Settings dismissal. New copy/styles remain lazy.

### Test-first development, retirement and honest feedback

Existing draft-navigation tests did not guard outer dismissal or aggregate independent form dirtiness. Two new cases first fail because Close loses fields without confirmation. An initial native `window.confirm` prototype passes two controls (14.4s) and build/types/Web **71/619**, but is superseded: blocking JavaScript cannot provide the required external retirement while confirmation is open. Two strengthened nonblocking-dialog cases retain policy REDs; the new inline confirmation then passes **2/2** (14.5s). These earlier prototypes are not current qualification.

The EN/FR × 1280/390px matrix plus delayed palette and external trust checks initially yields **5 PASS / 1 fast confirmation-Escape FAIL of 6** (33.4s). A sibling outside the inert Settings root does not satisfy the existing Escape stack's parent/child ownership. Nesting confirmation within Settings and making only header/business content inert yields **6 PASS** (28.0s), without changing the eager Escape stack or a timeout. Access/failed-save checks then yield **7 PASS / 1 incorrect test error-text FAIL of 8** (36.0s): the original API consumer exposes `HTTP 500`, not the server's synthetic detail. Corrected assertions retain that exact error and no reflected source; no product error handler changes. All eight pass (31.0s).

The original held-default retirement case first fails because its old helper expects unconditional Close. `closeSettings` now supports an explicit optional `discardDrafts` path requiring the actual named EN/FR confirmation and Discard button. Only that intentionally retiring default journey opts in; no blanket auto-discard is added. Original late-readback/no-notice/newer-draft assertions remain. The eight new controls plus this case pass **9/9** (32.6s).

Later source review identifies a missing value distinction: nonempty/save-eligible detection misses cleared configured keys, incomplete bullet-containing keys and cleared applied Access tokens. Two cases reproduce missing confirmation in a separate development container, without modifying the already-running preparatory owner. Private baseline comparison yields **2 PASS** (10.1s), then **15 PASS** (42.8s) across ten new cases and five existing administration cases. A focused pending-write assertion also rejects the initial promise that saved settings cannot change: the truthful in-flight-save warning yields **1 PASS** (7.4s), followed by **15 PASS** (42.3s). Discard does not undo already-admitted work; no save authority is weakened.

The ten new cases cover:

- EN/FR desktop/mobile aggregate credential/default flags, keyboard dismissal/Keep focus, cancellation, Tab containment, both 44px/3:1 focus controls, actual −0.1px clearance, axe, exact Project PATCH/key POST and independent successful-save cleanup;
- explicit Discard without extra writes or draft-secret storage, clean dismissal, cleared/incomplete values and Access Apply versus immediate browser preferences;
- held/failed credential writes retaining their dirty value and original `HTTP 500` feedback;
- actual held Dev palette import cancellation without prompting, no late reopen and subsequent Settings/confirmation Escape priority;
- source-browser trust withdrawal while confirmation is open, immediately replacing key/default values and removing old flags without a dialog or mutation. This dynamic store replacement is not emitted-Server/trust-transport or real-account proof.
